                If (avg_desc_len <= 792.5)
                 Predict: Pembelian Tinggi
                Else (avg_desc_len > 792.5)
                 Predict: Pembelian Rendah
               Else (freight_per_item > 15.635)
                Predict: Pembelian Tinggi
             Else (max_installments > 10.5)
              If (avg_weight_g <= 8283.5)
               Predict: Pembelian Rendah
              Else (avg_weight_g > 8283.5)
               Predict: Pembelian Tinggi
         Else (avg_desc_len > 988.5)
          If (avg_weight_g <= 5387.5)
           If (category in {pet_shop})
            Predict: Pembelian Rendah
           Else (category not in {pet_shop})
            Predict: Pembelian Tinggi
          Else (avg_weight_g > 5387.5)
           If (avg_width_cm <= 17.166)
            If (total_weight_g <= 9775.0)
             If (avg_desc_len <= 1138.4)
              Predict: Pembelian Rendah
             Else (avg_desc_len > 1138.4)
              Predict: Pembelian Tinggi
            Else (total_weight_g > 9775.0)
             Predict: Pembelian Tinggi
           Else (avg_width_cm > 17.166)
            Predict: Pembelian Tinggi
